238: function calcCashSummary(){
239:   const events=_cashEventsAll();
240:   let opening=0,contrib=0,draw=0,adjust=0,contribFY=0,drawFY=0,inflows=0,outflows=0,operatingCash=0;
241:   const fyS=(typeof _currentFYStart==='function')?_currentFYStart():new Date().getFullYear();const fyFrom=fyS+'-04-06',fyTo=(fyS+1)+'-04-05';
242:   const inFY=function(d){return d&&d>=fyFrom&&d<=fyTo;};
243:   events.forEach(function(e){const amt=Number(e.amount)||0;const signed=e.direction==='out'?-amt:amt;if(e.direction==='out')outflows+=amt;else inflows+=amt;
244:     if(e.type==='opening_balance')opening+=amt;else if(e.type==='owner_contribution'){contrib+=amt;if(inFY(e.date))contribFY+=amt;}else if(e.type==='owner_draw'){draw+=amt;if(inFY(e.date))drawFY+=amt;}else if(e.type==='adjustment')adjust+=signed;else operatingCash+=signed;});

248:   const stockTied=_cashStockTied();

250:   return {events:events,opening:+opening.toFixed(2),contrib:+contrib.toFixed(2),draw:+draw.toFixed(2),adjust:+adjust.toFixed(2),contribFY:+contribFY.toFixed(2),drawFY:+drawFY.toFixed(2),
251:     inflows:+inflows.toFixed(2),outflows:+outflows.toFixed(2),operatingCash:+operatingCash.toFixed(2),cashAvailable:cashAvailable,
252:     unpaidLiabilities:unpaidLiabilities,stockTied:stockTied,cashOverheads:_cashOverheadsAllTime(),capitalLost:_cashCapitalNetLost(),

254:     netOwner:+(draw-contrib).toFixed(2),profitRetained:+(netProfit-draw+contrib).toFixed(2)};

473: function calcSummaryDeltas(){
474:   const prevKey=_prevPeriodKey(SUMMARY_PERIOD);

479:   // / would read as +∞). For refund rate, lower is better → invert "good".
480:   const pct=function(c,p){
481:     if(p===null||p===undefined||Math.abs(p)<0.005)return null;
482:     return ((c-p)/Math.abs(p))*100;
483:   };
484:   const periodLabel=(SUMMARY_PERIOD==='current_fy')?'vs last FY'
485:     :(/^\d+d$/.test(SUMMARY_PERIOD)?('vs prev '+SUMMARY_PERIOD.replace('d',' days')):'vs prev period');
486:   return {
487:     label: periodLabel,
488:     totalRev:    pct(cur.totalRev,    prev.totalRev),
489:     grossProfit: pct(cur.grossProfit, prev.grossProfit),
490:     grossMargin: (prev.grossMargin!==undefined? (cur.grossMargin-prev.grossMargin):null), // pp change
491:     netProfit:   pct(cur.netProfit,   prev.netProfit),
492:     netMargin:   (prev.netMargin!==undefined? (cur.netMargin-prev.netMargin):null), // pp change
493:     soldCount:   pct(cur.soldCount,   prev.soldCount),
494:     refundItemRate: (cur.refundItemRate-prev.refundItemRate), // pp change; lower better
495:     avgProfit:   pct(cur.avgProfit,   prev.avgProfit)
496:   };
497: }

501: function _monthKeyFromDate(ds){
502:   if(!ds)return null;

516: function _currentResaleCycleNo(i){
517:   const relisted=((i&&i.returnHistory)||[]).filter(function(r){
518:     return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&r._relistedAt;
519:   });

526:     return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)>=2&&!!r._relistedAt;

529: function _failedCycleLoss(i,r){
530:   const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(r&&r.saleNo)||1);
531:   const isResale=saleNo>=2;
532:   const price=Math.max(0,Number(r&&r._salePriceAtReturn)||Number(r&&r._salePriceAtRelist)||0);
533:   const post=Math.max(0,Number(r&&r._postageAtReturn)!=null?Number(r._postageAtReturn):Number(r&&r._postageAtRelist)||0);
534:   const platformId=(r&&r._platformAtReturn)||(isResale?(i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i)):_s1Platform(i));
535:   const promoPct=Math.max(0,Number(r&&r._promoPercentAtReturn)!=null?Number(r._promoPercentAtReturn):Number(r&&r._promoPercentAtRelist)||0);
536:   // Freeze fulfilment against the completed sale. Newer return rows carry
537:   // *_AtReturn; legacy rows may only have the pre-relist snapshot, and very
538:   // old Sale 1 rows fall back to the item's original fulfilment values.
539:   const _maxFullSale=((i&&i.returnHistory)||[]).reduce(function(mx,x){return (x&&(x.type==='full_seller'||x.type==='full_ebay'))?Math.max(mx,Math.max(1,Number(x.saleNo)||1)):mx;},0);
540:   const _liveReturnedResale=(saleNo>=2&&i&&i.isReturned&&i.resaleSalePrice&&saleNo===_maxFullSale);
541:   const ship=Math.max(0,Number(r&&r._shippingCostAtReturn!=null?r._shippingCostAtReturn:(r&&r._shippingCostAtRelist!=null?r._shippingCostAtRelist:(_liveReturnedResale?(i.resaleShippingCost!=null?i.resaleShippingCost:i.shippingCost):(saleNo===1?i.shippingCost:0))))||0);
542:   const pkg=Math.max(0,Number(r&&r._packagingCostAtReturn!=null?r._packagingCostAtReturn:(r&&r._packagingCostAtRelist!=null?r._packagingCostAtRelist:(_liveReturnedResale?(i.resalePackagingCost!=null?i.resalePackagingCost:i.packagingCost):(saleNo===1?i.packagingCost:0))))||0);
543:   const fee=_netPlatformFeeAfterRefunds(i,saleNo,platformId,price,post);
544:   const promo=_netPromoFeeAfterRefunds(i,saleNo,platformId,price,post,promoPct);
545:   const outbound=_sellerPaysOutbound(platformId)?ship+pkg:0;
546:   const retPost=Math.max(0,Number(r&&r.returnPostage)||0);
547:   return +(fee+promo+outbound+retPost).toFixed(2);
548: }

572:     if(sellerHandled){

574:       platformCredit=c.eligibleRefundableGross*ratio;
575:       promoCredit=_calcPromoFee(platformId,price,post,promoPct)*ratio;
576:     }
577:   }else if(platformId==='depop'){
578:     // Depop refunds are modelled proportionally in RETRADE. Keep the credit on
579:     // the refund date rather than rewriting the original sale event.

715:   });});
716:   return out;
717: }

773: function _saleBreakdown(ev){
774:   const i=ev.item;
775:   if(ev.isReturnAdjustment){
776:     const r=ev.returnEntry||{};
777:     const impact=_returnEventImpact(i,r);
778:     const cr=_refundCreditsForEntry(i,r);
779:     const isFull=(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');
780:     // A full return physically restores the inventory basis. This is an
781:     // accounting COGS credit, not a cash inflow; Cashflow handles only refund,
782:     // fee-credit and return-postage movements.
783:     const itemCost=isFull?-(Number(i.costPrice)||0):0;
784:     const parts=isFull?-calcPartsCost(i):0;
785:     const totalCosts=+(impact.refund+impact.postage-impact.platformCredit-impact.promoCredit+itemCost+parts).toFixed(2);
786:     return{saleNo:'Sale '+Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1)+' return',platform:(PLATFORMS[cr.platformId]&&PLATFORMS[cr.platformId].short)||cr.platformId,
787:       salePrice:-impact.refund,postage:0,bpf:-impact.platformCredit,promoPct:0,promoFee:-impact.promoCredit,
788:       shipping:0,packaging:0,itemCost:+itemCost.toFixed(2),parts:+parts.toFixed(2),listingFee:0,
789:       returnRefund:impact.refund,returnPostage:impact.postage,partialRefund:0,partnerSplit:0,
790:       totalCosts:totalCosts,netProfit:+(-totalCosts).toFixed(2)};
791:   }
792:   const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(ev.sale)||1);
793:   const cyc=ev.snapshot||_saleCycleSnapshot(i,saleNo);
794:   if(!cyc)return{saleNo:'Sale '+saleNo,platform:'',salePrice:0,postage:0,bpf:0,promoPct:0,promoFee:0,shipping:0,packaging:0,itemCost:0,parts:0,listingFee:0,returnRefund:0,returnPostage:0,partialRefund:0,partnerSplit:0,totalCosts:0,netProfit:0};
795:   const salePrice=Math.max(0,Number(cyc.price)||0);
796:   const postageIncome=_sellerPostageIncome(cyc.platformId,cyc.postage);
797:   const shipping=Math.max(0,Number(cyc.shipping)||0);
798:   const packaging=Math.max(0,Number(cyc.packaging)||0);
799:   const promoPct=Math.max(0,Number(cyc.promoPercent)||0);
800:   const bpf=calcPlatformBPF(i,salePrice,cyc.postage||0,cyc.platformId);
801:   const promoFee=_calcPromoFee(cyc.platformId,salePrice,cyc.postage||0,promoPct);
802:   const itemCost=Math.max(0,Number(i.costPrice)||0),parts=Math.max(0,calcPartsCost(i));
803:   const listingFee=Math.max(0,Number(cyc.listingFee)||0);
804:   // Partner economic cost belongs to one sale cycle only. If the current sale
805:   // survives, attach it there. If the partner was already paid before a full
806:   // return, keep that sunk payout on the latest failed cycle instead of making
807:   // it disappear or charging it again on every resale.
808:   let partnerSplit=0,targetPartnerSale=null;
809:   try{
810:     const owed=_accountItemOwed(i);
811:     if(owed!=null&&Number(owed)>0){
812:       if(!i.isReturned){targetPartnerSale=(i.resaleSalePrice&&i.resaleDateSold)?_currentResaleCycleNo(i):1;}
813:       else if(i.accountSettled===true){const ns=_saleCycleNumbers(i);targetPartnerSale=ns.length?ns[ns.length-1]:1;}
814:       if(targetPartnerSale===saleNo)partnerSplit=Math.max(0,Number(owed)||0);
815:     }
816:   }catch(e){}
817:   const totalCosts=+(bpf+promoFee+shipping+packaging+itemCost+parts+listingFee+partnerSplit).toFixed(2);
818:   const revenueRecognised=+(salePrice+postageIncome).toFixed(2);
819:   const netProfit=+(revenueRecognised-totalCosts).toFixed(2);
820:   return{saleNo:'Sale '+saleNo,platform:(PLATFORMS[cyc.platformId]&&PLATFORMS[cyc.platformId].short)||cyc.platformId,
821:     salePrice:+salePrice.toFixed(2),postage:+postageIncome.toFixed(2),bpf:+bpf.toFixed(2),promoPct:+(promoPct*100).toFixed(1),promoFee:+promoFee.toFixed(2),
822:     shipping:+shipping.toFixed(2),packaging:+packaging.toFixed(2),itemCost:+itemCost.toFixed(2),parts:+parts.toFixed(2),listingFee:+listingFee.toFixed(2),
823:     returnRefund:0,returnPostage:0,partialRefund:0,partnerSplit:+partnerSplit.toFixed(2),totalCosts:totalCosts,netProfit:netProfit};
824: }

833:   // full amount the buyer paid). Shipping paid out to couriers is then a

1131:           listedCount:(DB[m]||[]).filter(function(i){return !i.dateSold&&!i.isReturned&&!i.resaleSalePrice&&!i.scrappedAt&&((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()!=='MONTH END');}).length};
1132: }
1133: 
1134: function calcEbayBPF(salePrice){
1135:   if(!salePrice||salePrice<=0)return 0;
1136:   const feeOnNet=function(net){
1137:     let f=0.10;
1138:     f+=0.07*Math.min(net,20);
1139:     if(net>20)f+=0.04*Math.min(net-20,280);
1140:     if(net>300)f+=0.02*Math.min(net-300,3700);
1141:     // eBay UK private Buyer Protection is capped at the £4,000 band.
1142:     // Any portion of the seller's item price above £4,000 adds no further BPF.
1143:     return f;
1144:   }
1145:   let net=salePrice;
1146:   for(let j=0;j<10;j++)net=salePrice-feeOnNet(net);
1147:   return+feeOnNet(net).toFixed(2);
1148: }

1151:   // Vinted: seller fee is £0. Buyer pays a separate Buyer Protection Fee on top.
===== accounting.js 1293-1357 =====
1293:     case 'vinted':    return calcVintedBPF(item,salePrice);
1294:     case 'depop':     return calcDepopBPF(item,salePrice,post);
1295:     case 'fb':        return calcFbBPF(item,salePrice);
1296:     case 'amazon_uk': return calcAmazonUKBPF(item,salePrice);
1297:     default:          return calcEbayBPF(salePrice+post);
1298:   }
1299: }

1301: function _calcPromoFee(platformId,itemPrice,buyerPostage,promoPercent){
1302:   const pct=Math.max(0,Number(promoPercent)||0);
1303:   if(!pct)return 0;
1304:   const price=Math.max(0,Number(itemPrice)||0);
1305:   const post=Math.max(0,Number(buyerPostage)||0);
1306:   let base=price;
1307:   if(platformId==='ebay'||platformId==='ebay_biz'||platformId==='depop')base+=post;
1308:   let fee=base*pct;
1309:   if(platformId==='ebay'||platformId==='ebay_biz')fee*=1.20;
1310:   return +fee.toFixed(2);
1311: }

1313: function _sellerRefundCreditRatio(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage){
1314:   const entries=(item&&item.returnHistory)||[];
1315:   const target=Math.max(1,Number(saleNo)||1);
1316:   // A seller-handled FULL refund represents a complete reversal for fee-credit
1317:   // purposes. eBay-stepped-in refunds deliberately do not qualify.
1318:   if(entries.some(function(r){
1319:     const rs=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
1320:     return rs===target&&r.type==='full_seller';
1321:   }))return 1;
1322:   const refunded=entries.filter(function(r){
1323:     const rs=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
1324:     return rs===target&&r.type==='partial_seller';
1325:   }).reduce(function(sum,r){return sum+(Number(r.refundAmount)||0);},0);
1326:   const total=Math.max(0,Number(salePrice)||0)+Math.max(0,Number(buyerPostage)||0);
1327:   if(total<=0||refunded<=0)return 0;
1328:   return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,refunded/total));
1329: }
1330: 
1331: function _netEbayBizFeeAfterRefunds(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage){
1332:   const total=Math.max(0,Number(salePrice)||0)+Math.max(0,Number(buyerPostage)||0);
1333:   const c=_calcEbayBizFeeComponents(total,item&&item.category,salePrice);
1334:   const ratio=_sellerRefundCreditRatio(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage);
1335:   const credit=c.eligibleRefundableGross*ratio;
1336:   return +Math.max(0,c.total-credit).toFixed(2);
1337: }

1339: function _allRefundRatio(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage){
1340:   const target=Math.max(1,Number(saleNo)||1);
1341:   const total=Math.max(0,Number(salePrice)||0)+Math.max(0,Number(buyerPostage)||0);
1342:   if(total<=0)return 0;
1343:   const refunded=((item&&item.returnHistory)||[]).filter(function(r){
1344:     const rs=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
1345:     return rs===target&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay'||r.type==='partial_seller'||r.type==='partial_ebay');
1346:   }).reduce(function(sum,r){return sum+(Number(r.refundAmount)||0);},0);
1347:   return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,refunded/total));
1348: }

1341:   const total=Math.max(0,Number(salePrice)||0)+Math.max(0,Number(buyerPostage)||0);
1342:   if(total<=0)return 0;
1343:   const refunded=((item&&item.returnHistory)||[]).filter(function(r){
1344:     const rs=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
1345:     return rs===target&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay'||r.type==='partial_seller'||r.type==='partial_ebay');
1346:   }).reduce(function(sum,r){return sum+(Number(r.refundAmount)||0);},0);
1347:   return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,refunded/total));
1348: }
1349: 
1350: function _netPlatformFeeAfterRefunds(item,saleNo,platformId,salePrice,buyerPostage){
1351:   const original=calcPlatformBPF(item,salePrice,buyerPostage,platformId);
1352:   if(!original)return 0;
1353:   if(platformId==='ebay_biz')return _netEbayBizFeeAfterRefunds(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage);
1354:   if(platformId==='depop'){
1355:     // Depop Payments reverses selling/payment/boost fees when a refund is
1356:     // processed. Partial refunds therefore receive the proportional share.
1357:     const ratio=_allRefundRatio(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage);
1358:     return +Math.max(0,original*(1-ratio)).toFixed(2);
1359:   }
1360:   // eBay Private BPF is paid by the BUYER, not the seller. RETRADE subtracts
1361:   // it only because private-eBay salePrice is stored as the buyer-facing item
1362:   // amount. Refunds do not turn it into a seller fee, so keep the original BPF
1363:   // conversion here; the buyer's prorated BPF refund is handled separately.
1364:   return original;
1365: }
1366: 
1367: function _netPromoFeeAfterRefunds(item,saleNo,platformId,salePrice,buyerPostage,promoPercent){
1368:   const original=_calcPromoFee(platformId,salePrice,buyerPostage,promoPercent);
1369:   if(!original)return 0;
1370:   let ratio=0;
1371:   if(platformId==='ebay'||platformId==='ebay_biz'){
1372:     ratio=_sellerRefundCreditRatio(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage);
1373:   }else if(platformId==='depop'){
1374:     ratio=_allRefundRatio(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage);
1375:   }
1376:   return +Math.max(0,original*(1-ratio)).toFixed(2);
1377: }

1331: function _netEbayBizFeeAfterRefunds(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage){
1332:   const total=Math.max(0,Number(salePrice)||0)+Math.max(0,Number(buyerPostage)||0);
1333:   const c=_calcEbayBizFeeComponents(total,item&&item.category,salePrice);
1334:   const ratio=_sellerRefundCreditRatio(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage);
1335:   const credit=c.eligibleRefundableGross*ratio;
1336:   return +Math.max(0,c.total-credit).toFixed(2);

1355:     // Depop Payments reverses selling/payment/boost fees when a refund is
1356:     // processed. Partial refunds therefore receive the proportional share.
1357:     const ratio=_allRefundRatio(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage);
1358:     return +Math.max(0,original*(1-ratio)).toFixed(2);
1359:   }
1360:   // eBay Private BPF is paid by the BUYER, not the seller. RETRADE subtracts
1361:   // it only because private-eBay salePrice is stored as the buyer-facing item
1362:   // amount. Refunds do not turn it into a seller fee, so keep the original BPF
1363:   // conversion here; the buyer's prorated BPF refund is handled separately.
1364:   return original;
1365: }

1350: function _netPlatformFeeAfterRefunds(item,saleNo,platformId,salePrice,buyerPostage){
1351:   const original=calcPlatformBPF(item,salePrice,buyerPostage,platformId);
1352:   if(!original)return 0;
1353:   if(platformId==='ebay_biz')return _netEbayBizFeeAfterRefunds(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage);
1354:   if(platformId==='depop'){
1355:     // Depop Payments reverses selling/payment/boost fees when a refund is
1356:     // processed. Partial refunds therefore receive the proportional share.
1357:     const ratio=_allRefundRatio(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage);
1358:     return +Math.max(0,original*(1-ratio)).toFixed(2);
1359:   }
1360:   // eBay Private BPF is paid by the BUYER, not the seller. RETRADE subtracts
1361:   // it only because private-eBay salePrice is stored as the buyer-facing item
1362:   // amount. Refunds do not turn it into a seller fee, so keep the original BPF
1363:   // conversion here; the buyer's prorated BPF refund is handled separately.
1364:   return original;
1365: }

1395: function _saleRefundTotals(item,saleNo){
1396:   const target=Math.max(1,Number(saleNo)||1);
1397:   const out={partialSeller:0,partialPlatform:0,fullSeller:0,fullPlatform:0,returnPostage:0,hasFull:false};
1398:   ((item&&item.returnHistory)||[]).forEach(function(r){
1399:     const rs=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
1400:     if(rs!==target)return;
1401:     const amt=Math.max(0,Number(r.refundAmount)||0);
1402:     out.returnPostage+=Math.max(0,Number(r.returnPostage)||0);
1403:     if(r.type==='partial_seller')out.partialSeller+=amt;
1404:     else if(r.type==='partial_ebay')out.partialPlatform+=amt;
1405:     else if(r.type==='full_seller'){out.fullSeller+=amt;out.hasFull=true;}
1406:     else if(r.type==='full_ebay'){out.fullPlatform+=amt;out.hasFull=true;}
1407:   });
1408:   Object.keys(out).forEach(function(k){if(typeof out[k]==='number')out[k]=+out[k].toFixed(2);});
1409:   return out;

1412: function _privateEbayBuyerFeeRefund(item,saleNo,salePrice,buyerPostage){
1413:   const bpf=calcEbayBPF(salePrice);
1414:   if(!bpf)return 0;
1415:   const target=Math.max(1,Number(saleNo)||1);
1416:   const refunded=((item&&item.returnHistory)||[]).filter(function(r){
1417:     const rs=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
1418:     return rs===target&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay'||r.type==='partial_seller'||r.type==='partial_ebay');
1419:   }).reduce(function(sum,r){return sum+(Number(r.refundAmount)||0);},0);
1420:   if(refunded<=0||salePrice<=0)return 0;
1421:   // eBay says the private Buyer Protection fee is refunded pro-rata with the
1422:   // item-price refund. It is eBay's money, not a seller cost. RETRADE's return
1423:   // form historically stores the buyer-facing refund amount, so remove the
1424:   // BPF share before treating that refund as seller cash outflow.
1425:   const ratio=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,refunded/Math.max(0.01,Number(salePrice)||0)));
1426:   return +(bpf*ratio).toFixed(2);
1427: }

1429: function calcPartsCost(i){
1430:   return (i.parts||[]).reduce(function(s,p){return s+(p.cost||0);},0);
1431: }

1433: function calcItemRevenue(i){

1437:   // Returned then resold — only the resale price counts as revenue
1438:   const _hadFullReturn=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(r=>r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');
1439:   const _wasReturnedThenResold=i.resaleSalePrice&&_hadFullReturn;

1376:   return +Math.max(0,original*(1-ratio)).toFixed(2);

1452: function calcGrossProfit(i){
1453:   if(!i.salePrice)return null;
1454:   // v1.3 — once an item has a real completed sale, realised item P&L is the
1455:   // chronological sum of immutable Sale-N and return events. This removes the
1456:   // old Sale-1/Sale-2 ceiling and stops a later relist from rewriting an older
1457:   // cycle. _saleBreakdown includes partner share for statement purposes, so add
1458:   // it back here: calcGrossProfit remains PRE-partner and calcNetProfit owns the
1459:   // single partner deduction contract.
1460:   const _actualCycles=(typeof _saleCycleNumbers==='function')?_saleCycleNumbers(i):[];
1461:   if(_actualCycles.length){
1462:     let _actualGross=0;
1463:     _actualCycles.forEach(function(n){
1464:       const cyc=_saleCycleSnapshot(i,n);if(!cyc)return;
1465:       const b=_saleBreakdown({item:i,sale:String(n),saleDate:cyc.date,salePrice:cyc.price,snapshot:cyc,isReturnAdjustment:false,isReturned:false});
1466:       _actualGross+=(Number(b.netProfit)||0)+(Number(b.partnerSplit)||0);
1467:     });
1468:     (i.returnHistory||[]).forEach(function(r){
1469:       const b=_saleBreakdown({item:i,sale:'R',saleDate:_returnEventDate(r),isReturnAdjustment:true,isReturned:true,returnEntry:r});
1470:       _actualGross+=(Number(b.netProfit)||0);
1471:     });
1472:     // A relisted-but-not-yet-resold item has incurred the NEXT insertion fee,
1473:     // but no Sale-N event exists yet to carry it. Recognise that sunk fee once.
1474:     if(!i.isReturned&&!i.dateSold&&!i.resaleSalePrice){
1475:       const pending=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&r._relistedAt;});
1476:       if(pending&&pending._listingFeeAtRelist!=null)_actualGross-=Math.max(0,Number(pending._listingFeeAtRelist)||0);
1477:     }
1478:     return +_actualGross.toFixed(2);
1479:   }
1480:   // F4 — a sale marked fully returned with NO return record (empty returnHistory)
1481:   // and no resale has zero revenue; without an entry to reverse it the code below
1482:   // reports phantom profit. Force 0 to match calcItemRevenue's isReturned rule.
1483:   if(i.isReturned && !i.resaleSalePrice && !(i.returnHistory||[]).length)return 0;
1484:   // ── Multi-cycle full returns ────────────────────────────────────────────
1485:   // When the item is physically back in stock after a full return, acquisition
1486:   // cost/parts remain recoverable stock. Lifetime realised P&L is therefore the
1487:   // sunk selling cost of each failed completed sale cycle. Sale 2+ cycles are
1488:   // frozen on their return rows before the live resale slot is reused.
1489:   const _fullRets=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
1490:   if(i.isReturned&&_fullRets.length){
1491:     let failed=0;
1492:     _fullRets.forEach(function(r){failed+=_failedCycleLoss(i,r);});
1493:     const listing=(Number(i.listingFee)||0)+(Number(i.resaleListingFee)||0);
1494:     return +(-(failed+listing)).toFixed(2);
1495:   }
1496:   // Partial refund handling for fees:

1480:   // F4 — a sale marked fully returned with NO return record (empty returnHistory)

1483:   if(i.isReturned && !i.resaleSalePrice && !(i.returnHistory||[]).length)return 0;
1484:   // ── Multi-cycle full returns ────────────────────────────────────────────
1485:   // When the item is physically back in stock after a full return, acquisition

1487:   // sunk selling cost of each failed completed sale cycle. Sale 2+ cycles are

1489:   const _fullRets=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
1490:   if(i.isReturned&&_fullRets.length){

1496:   // Partial refund handling for fees:
1497:   //   partial_seller (seller agreed): eBay refunds the fee difference — recalc BPF/promo on net sale price

1499:   //   Full returns are handled separately below (fee fully refunded on relist path)

1509:   // current values (slightly wrong but no worse than before).

1512:   const _snap=(_hasResale&&_fullReturnEntry)?_fullReturnEntry:null;

1516:   const _s1Packaging=(_snap&&_fullReturnEntry._packagingCostAtRelist!==undefined)?_fullReturnEntry._packagingCostAtRelist:(i.packagingCost||0);
1517:   const _s1Promo=(_snap&&_fullReturnEntry._promoPercentAtRelist!==undefined)?_fullReturnEntry._promoPercentAtRelist:(i.promoPercent||0);
1518:   const _baseSale=_snap?_s1SalePrice:i.salePrice;
1519:   const _s1PlatformId=_s1Platform(i);
1520:   // Canonical fee path: keep the ORIGINAL sale base intact, then apply any
1521:   // eligible refund credit to the fee itself. This is essential for banded

1552:     : _baseSale+_s1Postage-bpf-partialSellerRefund-partialEbayRefund+_s1PrivateBpfRefund;
1553:   const returnCost=(i.returnHistory||[]).reduce(function(s,r){
1554:     const fullReturn=(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');
1555:     const _rSale=(r.saleNo===2)?2:1;
1556:     if(fullReturn&&_rSale===1){
1557:       if(_wasRelisted){
1558:         // Sale principal already removed above; only return postage remains here.
1559:         return s+(r.returnPostage||0);
1560:       }
1561:       const refund=Math.max(0,Number(r.refundAmount)||0);
1562:       if(_s1PlatformId==='ebay'){
1563:         // Private eBay: the refund field is buyer-facing. eBay refunds its own
1564:         // Buyer Protection share, so only the seller-funded portion is cash out.
1565:         const buyerFeeRefund=_privateEbayBuyerFeeRefund(i,1,_baseSale,_s1Postage);
1566:         return s+Math.max(0,refund-buyerFeeRefund)+(r.returnPostage||0);
1567:       }
1568:       // Seller-fee platforms: fee credits are already reflected by `bpf` above;
1569:       // the refund principal itself is still paid back in full.
1570:       return s+refund+(r.returnPostage||0);
1571:     }
1572:     // Partial refunds: refund principal handled via revenue adjustment above.
1573:     // Only charge return postage here (refund amount itself is already netted out).
1574:     return s+(r.returnPostage||0);
1575:   },0);
1576:   const partsCost=calcPartsCost(i);
1577:   // Vinted (and any future buyer-pays-postage-only platform): seller pays nothing to ship
1578:   const _platForCost=_itemPlatform(i);
1579:   const _sellerPaysShipping=(_platForCost!=='vinted'&&_platForCost!=='fb');
1580:   // v2.19.26 — both listings' insertion fees. resaleListingFee is added here
1581:   // rather than inside the resale branch below so a relisted-but-unsold item
1582:   // still carries the fee it has already been charged.
1583:   const listingFee=(i.listingFee||0)+(i.resaleListingFee||0);
1584:   // v2.21.45 — a fully-returned item that hasn't been resold is BACK IN STOCK: its cost
1585:   // (buy + parts) is recoverable capital, not a realised loss. Only the sunk selling costs
1586:   // (shipping/pkg/promo/return/fees/listing) count. Resold items keep the cost (consumed by
1587:   // the resale, v2.21.16). Display-only — calcGrossProfit does NOT feed SA103.
1588:   const _backInStock=!!i.isReturned&&(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
1589:   const cost=(_backInStock?0:(i.costPrice||0))+(_sellerPaysShipping?_s1Shipping:0)+(_sellerPaysShipping?_s1Packaging:0)+promoCost+returnCost+(_backInStock?0:partsCost)+listingFee;
1590:   let profit=rev-cost;
1591:   // Second sale (resold after return)
1592:   if(i.resaleSalePrice){
1593:     const rPostage=i.resalePostage!=null?i.resalePostage:(i.postage||0);
1594:     const _rPlatformId=i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i);
1595:     const _liveResaleNo=_currentResaleCycleNo(i);
1596:     const rBPF=_netPlatformFeeAfterRefunds(i,_liveResaleNo,_rPlatformId,i.resaleSalePrice,rPostage);
1597:     const rPromoCost=_netPromoFeeAfterRefunds(i,_liveResaleNo,_rPlatformId,i.resaleSalePrice,rPostage,i.resalePromoPercent||0);
1598:     // v2.19.31 — partial refunds tagged to Sale 2. Principal is deducted here;
1599:     // any eligible fee/boost credit is already reflected in the canonical net
===== accounting.js 1575-1639 =====
1575:   },0);
1576:   const partsCost=calcPartsCost(i);
1577:   // Vinted (and any future buyer-pays-postage-only platform): seller pays nothing to ship
1578:   const _platForCost=_itemPlatform(i);
1579:   const _sellerPaysShipping=(_platForCost!=='vinted'&&_platForCost!=='fb');
1580:   // v2.19.26 — both listings' insertion fees. resaleListingFee is added here
1581:   // rather than inside the resale branch below so a relisted-but-unsold item
1582:   // still carries the fee it has already been charged.
1583:   const listingFee=(i.listingFee||0)+(i.resaleListingFee||0);
1584:   // v2.21.45 — a fully-returned item that hasn't been resold is BACK IN STOCK: its cost
1585:   // (buy + parts) is recoverable capital, not a realised loss. Only the sunk selling costs
1586:   // (shipping/pkg/promo/return/fees/listing) count. Resold items keep the cost (consumed by
1587:   // the resale, v2.21.16). Display-only — calcGrossProfit does NOT feed SA103.
1588:   const _backInStock=!!i.isReturned&&(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
1589:   const cost=(_backInStock?0:(i.costPrice||0))+(_sellerPaysShipping?_s1Shipping:0)+(_sellerPaysShipping?_s1Packaging:0)+promoCost+returnCost+(_backInStock?0:partsCost)+listingFee;
1590:   let profit=rev-cost;
1591:   // Second sale (resold after return)
1592:   if(i.resaleSalePrice){
1593:     const rPostage=i.resalePostage!=null?i.resalePostage:(i.postage||0);
1594:     const _rPlatformId=i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i);
1595:     const _liveResaleNo=_currentResaleCycleNo(i);
1596:     const rBPF=_netPlatformFeeAfterRefunds(i,_liveResaleNo,_rPlatformId,i.resaleSalePrice,rPostage);
1597:     const rPromoCost=_netPromoFeeAfterRefunds(i,_liveResaleNo,_rPlatformId,i.resaleSalePrice,rPostage,i.resalePromoPercent||0);
1598:     // v2.19.31 — partial refunds tagged to Sale 2. Principal is deducted here;
1599:     // any eligible fee/boost credit is already reflected in the canonical net
1600:     // fee helpers above.
1601:     const rPartialSeller=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(r=>r.type==='partial_seller'&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo).reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.refundAmount||0),0);
1602:     const rPartialEbay=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(r=>r.type==='partial_ebay'&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo).reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.refundAmount||0),0);
1603:     const _rPrivateBpfRefund=(_rPlatformId==='ebay')?(i.returnHistory||[])
1604:       .filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo&&(r.type==='partial_seller'||r.type==='partial_ebay');})
1605:       .reduce(function(sum,r){return sum+_refundCreditsForEntry(i,r).platformCredit;},0):0;
1606:     const _rFull=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');});
1607:     let _rFullSellerCash=0;
1608:     if(_rFull){
1609:       _rFullSellerCash=Math.max(0,Number(_rFull.refundAmount)||0);
1610:       if(_rPlatformId==='ebay')_rFullSellerCash=Math.max(0,_rFullSellerCash-_refundCreditsForEntry(i,_rFull).platformCredit);
1611:     }
1612:     const rRev=i.resaleSalePrice+rPostage-rBPF-(i.resaleShippingCost||0)-(i.resalePackagingCost||0)-rPromoCost
1613:       -rPartialSeller-rPartialEbay+_rPrivateBpfRefund-_rFullSellerCash;
1614:     profit+=rRev;
1615:   }
1616:   return+profit.toFixed(2);
1617: }

1619: function calcNetProfit(i){
1620:   const gross=calcGrossProfit(i);
1621:   if(gross===null)return null;
1622:   const owed=_accountItemOwed(i); // null if no split set, or item unsold
1623:   if(owed===null)return gross;
1624:   return+(gross-owed).toFixed(2);
1625: }

1627: function runFinancialRegressionTests(){
1628:   const near=function(a,b){return Math.abs((Number(a)||0)-(Number(b)||0))<0.011;};
1629:   const results=[];
1630:   const check=function(name,actual,expected){
1631:     const ok=near(actual,expected);
1632:     results.push({name:name,ok:ok,actual:+Number(actual).toFixed(2),expected:+Number(expected).toFixed(2)});
1633:     return ok;
1634:   };
1635:   const base=function(platform){return{
1636:     id:'TEST',item:'Regression item',defaultPlatform:platform,soldOnPlatform:platform,
1637:     salePlatform:platform,state:'sold',dateSold:'2026-08-01',salePrice:100,postage:0,
1638:     costPrice:40,shippingCost:4,packagingCost:1,promoPercent:0.02,
1639:     parts:[],listingFee:0,returnHistory:[]
1640:   };};
1641: 
1642:   // 1 — eBay private normal sale: buyer-facing item amount less BPF, costs + ad.
1643:   let i=base('ebay');

===== accounting.js 1582-1646 =====
1582:   // still carries the fee it has already been charged.
1583:   const listingFee=(i.listingFee||0)+(i.resaleListingFee||0);
1584:   // v2.21.45 — a fully-returned item that hasn't been resold is BACK IN STOCK: its cost
1585:   // (buy + parts) is recoverable capital, not a realised loss. Only the sunk selling costs
1586:   // (shipping/pkg/promo/return/fees/listing) count. Resold items keep the cost (consumed by
1587:   // the resale, v2.21.16). Display-only — calcGrossProfit does NOT feed SA103.
1588:   const _backInStock=!!i.isReturned&&(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
1589:   const cost=(_backInStock?0:(i.costPrice||0))+(_sellerPaysShipping?_s1Shipping:0)+(_sellerPaysShipping?_s1Packaging:0)+promoCost+returnCost+(_backInStock?0:partsCost)+listingFee;
1590:   let profit=rev-cost;
1591:   // Second sale (resold after return)
1592:   if(i.resaleSalePrice){
1593:     const rPostage=i.resalePostage!=null?i.resalePostage:(i.postage||0);
1594:     const _rPlatformId=i.resalePlatform||_itemPlatform(i);
1595:     const _liveResaleNo=_currentResaleCycleNo(i);
1596:     const rBPF=_netPlatformFeeAfterRefunds(i,_liveResaleNo,_rPlatformId,i.resaleSalePrice,rPostage);
1597:     const rPromoCost=_netPromoFeeAfterRefunds(i,_liveResaleNo,_rPlatformId,i.resaleSalePrice,rPostage,i.resalePromoPercent||0);
1598:     // v2.19.31 — partial refunds tagged to Sale 2. Principal is deducted here;
1599:     // any eligible fee/boost credit is already reflected in the canonical net
1600:     // fee helpers above.
1601:     const rPartialSeller=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(r=>r.type==='partial_seller'&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo).reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.refundAmount||0),0);
1602:     const rPartialEbay=(i.returnHistory||[]).filter(r=>r.type==='partial_ebay'&&(Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo).reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.refundAmount||0),0);
1603:     const _rPrivateBpfRefund=(_rPlatformId==='ebay')?(i.returnHistory||[])
1604:       .filter(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo&&(r.type==='partial_seller'||r.type==='partial_ebay');})
1605:       .reduce(function(sum,r){return sum+_refundCreditsForEntry(i,r).platformCredit;},0):0;
1606:     const _rFull=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(r){return (Number(r.saleNo)||1)===_liveResaleNo&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');});
1607:     let _rFullSellerCash=0;
1608:     if(_rFull){
1609:       _rFullSellerCash=Math.max(0,Number(_rFull.refundAmount)||0);
1610:       if(_rPlatformId==='ebay')_rFullSellerCash=Math.max(0,_rFullSellerCash-_refundCreditsForEntry(i,_rFull).platformCredit);
1611:     }
1612:     const rRev=i.resaleSalePrice+rPostage-rBPF-(i.resaleShippingCost||0)-(i.resalePackagingCost||0)-rPromoCost
1613:       -rPartialSeller-rPartialEbay+_rPrivateBpfRefund-_rFullSellerCash;
1614:     profit+=rRev;
1615:   }
1616:   return+profit.toFixed(2);
1617: }

1973: function calcCOGSOnly(i){
1974:   return +((i.costPrice||0)+calcPartsCost(i)).toFixed(2);
1975: }
1976: 
1977: function _estimatedPartnerCutFromProfit(i,profit){
1978:   if(i==null||profit==null)return 0;
1979:   if(_itemAccountType(i)==='supplier')return 0;
1980:   if(i.accountPaidAmount!=null)return +Math.max(0,Number(i.accountPaidAmount)||0).toFixed(2);
1981:   let pct=i.accountSplitPercent;
1982:   if(pct==null&&i.accountId){
1983:     const acct=(_accounts||[]).find(function(a){return a.id===i.accountId;});
1984:     if(acct&&acct.defaultSplitPercent!=null)pct=acct.defaultSplitPercent;
1985:   }
1986:   if(pct==null)return 0;
1987:   pct=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(pct)||0));
1988:   return +(Math.max(0,Number(profit)||0)*(pct/100)).toFixed(2);
1989: }
